   459	def activation_duplicate_provenance(result):
   460	    """Bind both actual OS processes and every native duplicate position receipt."""
   461	    try:
   462	        trial = result['Trials'][0]
   463	        primary, child, setup = trial['Actor'], trial['DuplicateActor'], trial['DuplicateSetup']
   464	        boot = result['BootIds']['Active']
   465	        if (primary['Pid'] == child['Pid'] or primary['Sid'] != child['Sid']
   466	                or primary['SessionId'] != child['SessionId'] or primary['BootId'] != boot
   467	                or child['BootId'] != boot or child['OwnerSid'] != child['Sid']
   468	                or child['Elevated'] is not False or child['IsAdministrator'] is not False
   469	                or not re.fullmatch(r'S-1-5-21-[0-9]+-[0-9]+-[0-9]+-[0-9]+', child['Sid'])
   470	                or not child['CommandLine'].endswith('duplicate-writer.ps1"')
   471	                or setup['SameFileObject'] is not True):
   472	            return False
   473	        receipts = [(setup['Duplicate'], primary), (setup['Adopt'], child),
   474	                    (setup['ParentPosition'], primary), (setup['ChildQuery'], child),
   475	                    (setup['ChildPosition'], child), (setup['ParentQuery'], primary)]
   476	        previous = 0
   477	        for receipt, actor in receipts:
   478	            if (receipt['Pid'] != actor['Pid'] or receipt['BootId'] != boot
   479	                    or type(receipt['NativeCode']) is not int or receipt['NativeCode'] != 0
   480	                    or type(receipt['StartQpc']) is not int or type(receipt['EndQpc']) is not int
   481	                    or not previous <= receipt['StartQpc'] <= receipt['EndQpc']):
   482	                return False
   483	            previous = receipt['EndQpc']
   484	        return (setup['Duplicate']['TargetPid'] == child['Pid']
   485	                and setup['Duplicate']['SourceHandle'] > 0
   486	                and setup['Duplicate']['RemoteHandle'] > 0
   487	                and setup['Duplicate']['RemoteHandle'] == setup['Adopt']['RemoteHandle']
   488	                and setup['ParentPosition']['Position'] == setup['ChildQuery']['Position'] == 317
   489	                and setup['ChildPosition']['Position'] == setup['ParentQuery']['Position'] == 619)
   490	    except (KeyError, TypeError, ValueError, IndexError):
   776	        if result.get('CaseId') in ('A01', 'A02', 'A03', 'A04'):
   777	            required = A04_REQUIRED_ASSERTIONS if result['CaseId'] == 'A04' else ACTIVATION_REQUIRED_ASSERTIONS
   778	            missing = required - {a.get('Name') for a in assertions}
   779	            blockers.update('Missing:' + name for name in missing)
   780	            if result['CaseId'] == 'A04' and not activation_duplicate_provenance(result):
   781	                blockers.add('DuplicateActorProvenance')
   782	        assertions = assertions + predicate.get('Assertions', [])
   783	        classes = [c.get('Class') for c in trial.get('Latency', [])]
   784	        latency_ok = mvp_latency_passed(latency_evidence, result, classes)
   785	        for assertion in assertions:
   786	            name, verdict = assertion.get('Name', ''), assertion.get('Verdict')
   787	            if verdict == 'PASS':
   788	                continue
   789	            allowed = (name in MVP_DEFERRED_EXACT or
   790	                       bool(name[:-len('PublicationAndTemporalCoverage')]) and name.endswith('PublicationAndTemporalCoverage') or
   791	                       bool(name[:-len('UnheldLatency')]) and name.endswith('UnheldLatency') and latency_ok)
   792	            if verdict == 'INCONCLUSIVE' and allowed:
   793	                deferred.add(name)
   794	            else:
   795	                blockers.add(name or 'UnnamedAssertion')

  2853	function Test-ActivationChildWindow($Trial,$Before,$After,$Mutation,$HandBackBefore,$HandBackAfter,[string]$Target,$Primary,$Child) {
  2854	    $window=$Before.Status -ceq 'OK' -and $After.Status -ceq 'OK' -and $Before.BootId -ceq $After.BootId -and
  2855	        $Before.EndQpc -le $Mutation.StartQpc -and $Mutation.EndQpc -le $After.StartQpc
  2856	    $delta=Test-ServiceJournalDelta $Before $After $window
  2857	    $exact=@($delta.NewEntries | Where-Object {$_.Entry.Transfer.DestinationPath -ieq $Target -and [int]$_.Entry.Transfer.ProcessId -in @([int]$Primary.Pid,[int]$Child.Pid)})
  2858	    $verdict=if($exact.Count -or $delta.Findings.Count){'FAIL'}elseif($delta.Complete){'PASS'}else{'INCONCLUSIVE'}
  2859	    Add-ActivationAssertion $Trial 'NoJournalDuringChildMutation' $verdict 'Pre-protection child physical mutation allocates no new exact destination transfer or service release/hand-back state; actual authenticated journal delta required.' @{Before=$Before;After=$After;Delta=$delta;Exact=$exact}
  2860	    $known=$HandBackBefore.Sid -ceq $Child.Sid -and $HandBackAfter.Sid -ceq $Child.Sid -and $HandBackBefore.Root -ceq $HandBackAfter.Root -and
  2861	        $HandBackBefore.BootId -ceq $Child.BootId -and $HandBackAfter.BootId -ceq $Child.BootId -and $HandBackBefore.Qpc -le $Mutation.StartQpc -and $HandBackAfter.Qpc -ge $Mutation.EndQpc

## REJECT

The A04 increment is not harness-ready. This review does not claim qualification.

### Findings

1. **[Blocker] A04 can stop before the child mutation.** The A04 path calls `Get-ActivationHandBackInventory` before issuing `write-old`; that function throws unless it finds exactly one profile for the actor SID ([suite.ps1:2846](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:2846), [suite.ps1:3135](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3135)). Preparation creates a profile only when `$cachedCase` is true, which excludes A04 ([suite.ps1:3561](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3561)). The file’s own comment says batch-logon tasks do not create a loaded profile. That leaves the inventory call able to abort before the child write stimulus.

2. **[Blocker] The final-cleanup check compares different clocks.** The Inspector trace timestamp comes from `KeQuerySystemTime` ([StageStream.c:233](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/SafeUpload.Minifilter/StageStream.c:233)); the release receipt uses `Stopwatch.GetTimestamp()`. The comparison at [suite.ps1:3212](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3212) therefore cannot establish that cleanup occurred within the release interval. In practice, the trace time is far larger than the QPC end value, so `ChildLastCloseExactlyOneCleanup` cannot pass even for a valid close. That assertion is required for A04. The in-diff plan audit also identifies this mismatch ([MVP-PLAN.md:1234](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/MVP-PLAN.md:1234)).

3. **[High] The standard-user actor can alter the command and receipt channel.** Preparation grants the actor Modify access to the actor directory ([suite.ps1:3573](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3573)); the duplicate actor directory is created beneath it ([suite.ps1:3675](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3675)). The coordinator reads identity and native-operation replies from these directories, while the Python gate checks their claimed values and ordering, not their origin ([suite.ps1:818](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:818), [qualification.py:459](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Invoke-StagedInvariantQualification.py:459)). Under the stated adversary model, the actor can pre-create or modify reply files and alter commands. In particular, `CrossProcessSameFileObject` relies on actor-reported duplicate and shared-position receipts.

4. **[High] A failed raw sample can produce a PASS for “unchanged.”** The A04 approval branch sets `PostPromotionRawDestinationUnchanged` from the difference result alone ([suite.ps1:2916](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:2916)). `Get-ActivationRawDifference` reads images from `Sample.Captures` but does not require `Sample.Status` to be `OK` ([suite.ps1:1017](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:1017)). The observer preserves captures and partial images in an error sample ([observer.psm1:1007](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/StagedInvariantObserver.psm1:1007)). If an errored or incoherent capture contains matching extents, this path can report zero differences and PASS. The A04 raw comparison should require a successful complete sample and validate the retained artifacts.

5. **[High] The Python MVP gate does not require the temporal deferral to be present.** `A04_REQUIRED_ASSERTIONS` omits `A04PublicationAndTemporalCoverage` and `NeverReadyWholeHolderInterval` ([qualification.py:451](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Invoke-StagedInvariantQualification.py:451)). The MVP gate checks only listed assertions and allows inconclusive assertions only when present ([qualification.py:776](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Invoke-StagedInvariantQualification.py:776)). The new synthetic positive fixture contains no temporal deferral yet expects `MvpGatePassed=True` ([test_staged_a04_gate.py:21](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/test_staged_a04_gate.py:21), [test_staged_a04_gate.py:29](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/test_staged_a04_gate.py:29)). Thus missing whole-window coverage can disappear from the MVP assessment instead of remaining recorded as INCONCLUSIVE.

6. **[Medium] The child-window checks can PASS despite contrary evidence.** `NoJournalDuringChildMutation` filters new exact-destination transfers to only the primary and child PIDs ([suite.ps1:2857](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:2857)); a valid transfer for that destination from another PID is ignored. `NoHandBackDuringChildMutation` returns PASS from empty before/after inventories, despite acknowledging that transient emissions are not covered ([suite.ps1:2860](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:2860)). These are edge snapshots, not complete interval evidence.

7. **[Medium] The parent-close checkpoint underchecks its stated contract.** It requires that `openerPids` contain the primary PID, but does not require that the child PID be absent; it also omits checks for `S=NO`, `C=0`, and `T=0` ([suite.ps1:3091](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3091)). The modified plan says the opener should remain the original process only and calls for exact live-holder counters ([MVP-PLAN.md:1223](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/MVP-PLAN.md:1223), [MVP-PLAN.md:1241](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/MVP-PLAN.md:1241)). As written, this assertion can pass with extra opener or counter evidence.

8. **[Medium] The primary actor’s task limit is shorter than the A04 bounded waits.** The primary task is limited to five minutes ([suite.ps1:3672](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3672)). The sequential A04 bounds include 45 seconds for epoch stabilization, 45 for Pending, 90 for promotion, 60 for Ready, 15 for held allocation, and 120 for release ([suite.ps1:3041](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3041), [suite.ps1:3063](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3063), [suite.ps1:2920](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:2920), [suite.ps1:2936](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:2936)). Those bounds total 375 seconds before overhead, so a slow but still in-bound run can lose the primary actor before the approval stimulus or cleanup completes.

### Test and status gaps

The new gate test is untracked and omitted from the qualification provenance pin list ([qualification.py:177](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Invoke-StagedInvariantQualification.py:177)). It also does not test the error-sample raw comparison or the temporal-assertion omission; its positive fixture currently encodes the latter as acceptable.

The case table marks A04 `Ready` ([StagedInvariantCases.psd1:173](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/StagedInvariantCases.psd1:173)), while the plan says the implementation remains NotReady and approval/coverage work is still being added ([MVP-PLAN.md:1219](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/MVP-PLAN.md:1219)). Reconcile that status before treating the row as ready.

I did not run tests or VMs. I treated the reported Windows parser/proof/native results and Python negative-control results as supplied context; the genuine native two-process control is still underway, and actual A04 debuggee runtime evidence remains outstanding.

The modified plan says the opener should remain the original process only and calls for exact live-holder counters ([MVP-PLAN.md:1223](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/MVP-PLAN.md:1223), [MVP-PLAN.md:1241](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/MVP-PLAN.md:1241)). As written, this assertion can pass with extra opener or counter evidence.

8. **[Medium] The primary actor’s task limit is shorter than the A04 bounded waits.** The primary task is limited to five minutes ([suite.ps1:3672](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3672)). The sequential A04 bounds include 45 seconds for epoch stabilization, 45 for Pending, 90 for promotion, 60 for Ready, 15 for held allocation, and 120 for release ([suite.ps1:3041](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3041), [suite.ps1:3063](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:3063), [suite.ps1:2920](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:2920), [suite.ps1:2936](/home/victor/Work/safeupload-wt-sol/driver/scripts/Test-StagedInvariantSuite.ps1:2936)). Those bounds total 375 seconds before overhead, so a slow but still in-bound run can lose the primary actor before the approval stimulus or cleanup completes.
